Scope of Work – Sky Hi Drainage Channel 
Improvement Project 
Navajo County, Arizona 
 
 
Atkins North America, Inc. (ATKINS) is pleased to submit this proposal for engineering consulting 
services to Navajo County for the Sky Hi Drainage Channel Impro vement Project in Navajo 
County, Arizona. The proposed improvements consist of approxima tely 3,200 feet of drainage 
channel reconstruction (further described below in this scope o f work). Project limits are shown 
below in Figure 1. 
 
Figure 1 – Project Limits

<!-- page 629 -->
Scope of Work 
Sky Hi Drainage Channel Improvements 
 
   
Based on our current understanding of the project, ATKINS will provide the following scope of 
services necessary to design the above-described proposed improvements.  
 
I. SCOPE OF SERVICES 
TASK 100 – PROJECT MANAGEMENT AND MEETINGS 
A. Meetings:  ATKINS anticipates having a kickoff meeting with Navajo County (also referred 
to as “the County” elsewhere in this proposal), as well as review meetings with the County 
at sixty (60), and ninety (90) percent completion, for a total of three (3) meetings. Up to 
two (2) additional as-needed meetings are included. These meeti ngs are assumed to 
occur via conference call, with the exception of the 60 percent  completion meeting which 
will be held at the project site. 
B. Project Management:  This task includes the traditional proj ect management 
responsibilities associated with this type of project including  day-to-day coordination with 
the County; administration of the contract, including invoicing  and budget and schedule 
management; and coordination with project staff and subconsultants.   
C. QC/QC Review:  ATKINS will perform internal quality control reviews of all design and 
documents prior to each milestone submittal.  In addition, seni or ATKINS staff will be 
involved with key issues at stra tegic times during the design p rocess.  All work will be 
subjected to ATKINS’ internal formal QA/QC program to ensure quality and completeness 
of all deliverables prior to submittal to the County. 
TASK 200 – SURVEY AND UTILITY COORDINATION 
A. ATKINS will coordinate with the local utility companies to d etermine the location(s) of 
the companies’ respective utility line(s) within the project’s limits of disturbance.  Any 
available facility mapping will be incorporated into the design  documents.  It is not 
anticipated that potholing of existing utility lines will be re quired, and these services 
are excluded from this scope of services.  
B. Existing survey has been performed for the project area, whi ch has been previously 
provided to ATKINS. It is assumed that this information is suff icient for the design. If 
additional localized survey is required, it is assumed that the  County will provide 
surveying services for the project. Additionally, the County will provide the right-of-way 
and easement locations in electronic format. No additional righ t-of-way or surveying 
services will be provided. 
TASK 300 – FIELD WORK AND DESIGN 
ATKINS will perform field work and design investigation services in support of the final design.  
The design investigation services will include the following tasks: 
A. Field Site Review and Visits:  ATKINS will perform one field  site visit after 60% design 
plans have been completed to observe and confirm existing condi tions as applicable to 
the project design.  The field visit will be conducted with County staff to review the project 
site, tabulate an inventory of existing improvements, and discu ss the proposed 
improvements.

<!-- page 630 -->
Scope of Work 
Sky Hi Drainage Channel Improvements 
 
   
B. Geotechnical Services:  Geotechnical services will not be pr ovided for this project. 
Existing work in the area has deemed the hard soils underlying the channel 
improvement locations to be “rippable” and able to be excavated with standard 
equipment (no blasting required). This will be assumed to be the case for the entire 
alignment. 
C. Hydrology, Hydraulics, and Scour Analysis:   
Hydrology Analysis:  Hydrology has been previously determined for the project and no 
new hydrology will be performed. 
Hydraulic Analysis:  Hydraulic calculations were previously performed for the pro ject. 
Proposed improvements will be designed for an approximate 20 to  30-foot bottom 
width or approximately 10- to 25-year return interval (to be de termined during actual 
design). The previously-prepared HEC-RAS model will be modified  to create a 
proposed-condition model that matches the improvement design. 
Scour Analysis :  Scour analyses will not be performed. Any scour protection 
improvements will be specified using standard protection design s but will not be 
designed for specific flows or return intervals. It is assumed that the competent 
material will provide some level of scour resistance and the channel bottom will not be 
lined. 
 
D. Design memo:  The limited hydrology and hydraulics (H&H) ana lyses described 
above will be documented in a brief technical memorandum. 
TASK 400 – FINAL DESIGN 
ATKINS will prepare improvement plans, specifications and a cos t estimate (PS&E) for the 
above-described proposed improvements.  The PS&E will be based on the Maricopa 
Association of Governments (MAG) Uniform Standard Specification s and Details for Public 
Works Construction; Arizona Department of Transportation (ADOT)  standard specifications 
and details; and/or the County’s manuals and details. 
A. Improvement Plans:  The final set of improvement plans will consist of the following sheets: 
 Cover sheet 
 Construction notes, legend and abbreviations sheet 
 Channel plan and profile sheets 
 Details and cross-section sheet(s) with applicable details and  t y p i c a l  c r o s s -
sections (e.g. channel cross-sections, culvert pipe(s), headwal ls, wing walls, 
permanent erosion control measures, etc.).   
It is not anticipated that landscape inventory/salvage sheets, landscape planting/re-seeing 
sheets, SWPPP, signage, or traffic control sheets will be required, and are excluded from 
this scope of services. 
B. Special Provisions:  ATKINS will prepare the special provisi ons for construction of the 
proposed improvements.  The special provisions will supplement the MAG specifications, 
and will address special items related to the project such as r elocation of utilities, work 
restrictions, special requirements imposed by the County, definitions and terms, materials 
not covered in the MAG specs, etc.

<!-- page 631 -->
Scope of Work 
Sky Hi Drainage Channel Improvements 
 
   
It is anticipated that the County will provide contract documen ts (e.g. general conditions, 
instructions to bidders, bonding and insurance requirements, ad vertisement for bid, 
bidding requirements, bid schedule, contract requirements, DBE requirements, award of 
contract, progress payments to contractor, payment retention, e t c . )  f o r  u s e  i n  t h e  b i d  
package. 
C. Engineer’s Opinion of Probable Cost:  ATKINS will prepare a quantity takeoff of the 
proposed improvements and from that develop an engineer’s opini on of probable cost 
(OPC) – also referred to colloquially as a “cost estimate” – for construction of the proposed 
improvements.  The OPC will be prepared in a format matching th e methods of 
measurement and payment outlined in the MAG specifications. 
D. Submittals:  ATKINS will submit the PS&Es in accordance with  the following schedule of 
elements: 
60% Submittal: 
The 60% submittal will include the following elements: 
 Improvement plans – ATKINS will submit plans at an approximate ly 60% level 
of completion.  The plans will include a cover sheet, standard notes, P&P 
sheets, and details. 
 Cover sheet and General Notes – ATKINS will prepare a cover sh eet and notes 
sheets for the project.  All standard notes will be included; s pecial notes and 
special details will be developed in a later stage. 
 Preliminary horizontal plans – ATKINS will develop preliminary  alignments and 
plans for the proposed improvements.  To the degree possible (f rom 
preliminary information), all utility crossings, scour depth an d other controlling 
features shall be annotated in the profile window.   
 Standard and special details – ATKINS will include any anticip ated standard 
details, and well as anticipated special details and cross-sections. 
 Engineer’s OPC – ATKINS will prepare an engineer’s OPC based o n the 60% 
plans. 
 Special provisions – ATKINS will create special provisions to address 
recommended changes and clarifications to the standard MAG specifications. 
90% Submittal (Pre-final): 
The 90% submittal will include the following elements: 
 Pre-final Improvement plans – ATKINS will submit plans at an a pproximately 
90% level of completion.  The plans will include a cover sheet, standard notes, 
P&P sheets, and details.   
 Standard and special details – ATKINS will include any anticip ated standard 
details, and well as anticipated special details and cross sections. 
 Deliverables include: PDF electronic version and two (2) full- size and two (2) 
half-size draft sets of engineering plans. 
 Pre-final design memorandum – ATKINS will prepare a design mem orandum 
summarizing the H&H analysis used to design the improvements.

<!-- page 632 -->
Scope of Work 
Sky Hi Drainage Channel Improvements 
 
   
 Pre-final engineer’s OPC – A TKINS will prepare an engineer’s OPC based on 
the 90% improvement plans. 
 Pre-final special provisions – ATKINS will prepare special pro visions, revised 
in accordance with the County’s review of the 60% submittal and  updated as 
necessary to reflect the 90% design. 
100% Submittal (Final): 
Our 100% submittal will include the following elements: 
 Cover sheet and General Notes – ATKINS will prepare a cover sh eet and notes
